id="_R_c35crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>langchain</code> v1.5.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">LangChain 1.5 adds decision models and decision-driven middleware. This entry also highlights recent improvements across core and integration packages since LangChain 1.4.0.</span><ul>
 <li><strong>Python 3.11 or later required</strong>: <code>langchain</code> 1.5 and <code>langchain-core</code> 1.8 drop Python 3.10 support. Upgrade Python before upgrading these packages.</li>
 <li><strong>Decisions API</strong>: <code>langchain_core.decisions</code> introduces <code>BaseDecisionModel</code> for typed, probabilistic answers about text, messages, and structured state. Questions support binary judgments (<code>Noul</code>), categorical choices (<code>Choice</code>), ordinal scores (<code>Score</code>), and bounding boxes (<code>Box</code>). (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchain/pull/41765" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">langchain#41765</a>)</li>
 <li><strong>Decision-driven middleware (beta)</strong>: <code>ModelRouterMiddleware</code> selects a model for each run from your configured choices and instructions. <code>AutoModeMiddleware</code> classifies configured tool calls and blocks those it judges risky. Versions of these middleware were previously released in <code>langchain-typesafe</code>. They now require an explicit decision model through <code>classifier=</code>. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchain/pull/41766" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">langchain#41766</a>)</li>
@@ -1126,7 +1126,7 @@
 <li><strong>Summarization improvements</strong>: <code>SummarizationMiddleware</code> accepts a separate model or custom callable through <code>summarizer=</code>.</li>
 <li><strong>Mid-conversation updates</strong>: Supported models accept <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/anthropic#mid-conversation-system-messages" class="link">Anthropic system and tool updates</a>, <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/openai#add-tools-mid-conversation" class="link">OpenAI Responses tool additions</a>, and <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/openai#changing-reasoning-effort-mid-conversation" class="link">OpenAI reasoning-effort changes</a> without restarting the conversation.</li>
 </ul></div></div></div>

The <a href="/langsmith/agent-server" class="link">Agent Server</a> passes events through unchanged, so the <code>langchain-core</code> version on the server decides which events clients receive.</span><ol>

 <li>Upgrade every client that reads the stream:
 <ul>
 <li>In JavaScript, upgrade <code>@langchain/react</code>, <code>@langchain/vue</code>, <code>@langchain/svelte</code>, or <code>@langchain/angular</code> to 1.2.6 or later.</li>
@@ -1135,13 +1135,13 @@
 </li>
 <li>Upgrade <code>langchain-core</code> on the server to 1.7.0 or later, together with <code>langchain</code> 1.4.7 or later.</li>
 </ol><span data-as="p"><code>langchain</code> and <code>deepagents</code> accept any <code>langchain-core</code> 1.x, so upgrading either one can pull in 1.7.0. To hold the server back until you upgrade every client, pin <code>langchain-core&lt;1.7.0</code>.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="breaking-changes" aria-labelledby="_R_1s55crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#breaking-changes" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Breaking changes">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1s55crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Breaking changes</span></h3><span data-as="p">Starting in <code>langchain-core</code> 1.6.13, iterating <code>message.tool_calls</code> yields only the new part of each tool call’s arguments, with the call’s <code>id</code> and <code>name</code>. Earlier versions yielded all of the arguments so far. A call’s chunks concatenate into its full arguments, and <code>message.tool_calls.get()</code> still returns the parsed tool calls.</span></div></div></div>

 </code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#eff1f5;--fade-color-dark:#1e1e2e"></div></div><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features" aria-labelledby="_R_1475crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1475crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>Typed answers</strong>: each interrupt carries a <code>response_schema</code> listing the decisions that call allows, so a client can build its form from the schema. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#read-the-response-schema" class="link">Read the response schema</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Checked before saving</strong>: LangGraph rejects an invalid answer before it is saved, so you can correct it and answer the same interrupt again. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#correct-an-invalid-answer" class="link">Correct an invalid answer</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Edits stay on the called tool</strong>: an <code>edit</code> can’t switch tools, and its arguments are checked against the tool’s own schema.</li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Use per-call mode for new agents. LangChain plans to make it the default in the next major release. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#pause-once-per-tool-call" class="link">Pause once per tool call</a> and the <a href="/oss/python/concepts/human-in-the-loop" class="link">human-in-the-loop overview</a>.</span></div></div></div>

Importing from it raises a <code>LangChainBetaWarning</code>.</span></div></div><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-2" aria-labelledby="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features-2" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>One adapter, any transport</strong>: <code>MCPAdapter</code> infers the transport from its target — a URL, a local script over stdio, an in-process server, an <code>MCPConfig</code> dict for <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/connections#multiple-servers" class="link">multiple servers</a>, or a pre-built <code>fastmcp.Client</code>. <code>await adapter.list_tools()</code> returns LangChain tools ready for <code>create_agent</code>. See the <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp" class="link">MCP overview</a>.</li>
@@ -1149,7 +1149,7 @@
 <li><strong>Authentication through FastMCP</strong>: bearer tokens, full OAuth 2.1 with dynamic client registration, or any <code>httpx.Auth</code>, including <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth#per-user-authentication" class="link">per-user authentication</a> in a deployment. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth" class="link">Authentication</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Richer tool metadata</strong>: each tool carries its MCP provenance under an <code>mcp</code> namespace on its metadata, including annotations such as <code>destructive_hint</code> for <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#human-in-the-loop" class="link">gating destructive tools behind approval</a>. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#tool-metadata" class="link">Tool metadata</a>.</li>
 </ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="migrating" aria-labelledby="_R_1s95crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#migrating" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Migrating">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1s95crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Migrating</span></h3><span data-as="p">Moving from <code>langchain-mcp-adapters</code>? <code>MultiServerMCPClient</code> is replaced by <code>MCPAdapter</code>, and several features have changed or been removed. See the <a href="/oss/python/migrate/langchain-mcp-adapters" class="link">migration guide</a>.</span></div></div></div>

id="_R_cb5crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>deepagents</code> v0.7.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">A leaner, more configurable harness by default. On a default-agent turn, input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895), validated against our <a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/how-we-benchmark-deep-agents" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">revamped evaluation suite</a> with no quality regression.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="optimizations" aria-labelledby="_R_sb5crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#optimizations" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Optimizations">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_sb5crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Optimizations</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>Lean prompts by default</strong>: The authored base prompt starts empty and tool-usage prose that duplicated tool schemas has been trimmed. Isolated to the default agent’s tool schemas, total description tokens drop <strong>43%</strong> (4,005 → 2,302); combined with the empty base prompt and opt-in todos, a default-agent turn’s input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895). Tool behavior is unchanged. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4859" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4859</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4979" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4979</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/5009" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#5009</a>)</li>
 </ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-3" aria-labelledby="_R_1cb5crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features-3" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1cb5crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/customization#middleware" class="link">Override a default middleware instance</a></strong>: A <code>middleware=</code> (or subagent <code>middleware</code>) instance whose <code>.name</code> matches a built-in now replaces that default in place, rather than erroring on a duplicate. For example, pass your own <code>SummarizationMiddleware(...)</code> to change the token trigger or summary model without disabling the built-in default. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4251" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4251</a>)</li>
@@ -1161,7 +1161,7 @@
 <li><strong>Backend compatibility shims removed</strong>: Pass concrete <code>BackendProtocol</code> instances instead of factories, configure <code>StoreBackend</code> with an explicit <code>namespace</code>, and use the current <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> / <code>grep</code> / <code>ReadResult</code> APIs. Removed symbols include <code>BackendFactory</code>, <code>BACKEND_TYPES</code>, <code>FileFormat</code>, and <code>Unset</code>. New files store string <code>FileData.content</code>; older <code>list[str]</code> content stays readable and converts on next write. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4541" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4541</a>)</li>
 <li><strong>Output format changes</strong>: Empty <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> output is now <code>No files found</code> instead of <code>[]</code>, and <code>read_file</code> no longer renders a fixed-width <code>cat -n</code>-style gutter — update any parsers of raw tool output. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4561" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4561</a>)</li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Copy the following prompt into your AI coding assistant to migrate a codebase for these breaking changes:</span><div class="prompt my-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-4 rounded-2xl border p-4 border-stone-100 bg-stone-50 dark:border-stone-800 dark:bg-stone-900 text-sm/[20px] text-stone-900 tracking-[-0.1px] dark:text-stone-50 print:print-color-exact"><div class="not-prose flex min-w-0 items-start gap-2" data-component-part="prompt-content-wrapper"><span aria-hidden="true" class="flex h-[1lh] shrink-0 select-none items-center justify-center" data-component-part="prompt-icon"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! id="_R_cd5crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>deepagents</code> v0.6.0</span></h2><ul>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/interpreters" class="link"><code>CodeInterpreterMiddleware</code></a></strong>: (experimental) <code>deepagents</code> now supports code execution and programmatic tool calling through a scoped QuickJS runtime.</li>
 <li>Supports <code>version=&quot;v3&quot;</code> in <code>stream_events</code> / <code>astream_events</code>. Refer to the <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/event-streaming" class="link">event streaming</a> guide for details.</li>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel" class="link"><code>DeltaChannel</code></a> (beta)</strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/delta-channels-evolving-agent-runtime" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">blog</a>): Deep Agents now uses <code>DeltaChannel</code> for message history and agent files. Rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value into every checkpoint, only the incremental delta written at each step is stored — keeping checkpoint sizes small as threads grow long.</li>
@@ -1169,8 +1169,8 @@
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/profiles" class="link">Harness profiles</a></strong>: Register per-provider or per-model configuration bundles (<code>HarnessProfile</code>) that <code>create_deep_agent</code> applies automatically when a model is selected — system-prompt tweaks, tool overrides, middleware changes, and subagent defaults — without modifying the call site.</li>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/backends#contexthubbackend" class="link"><code>ContextHubBackend</code></a></strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/introducing-context-hub" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">blog</a>): A new filesystem backend backed by LangSmith Hub. Agent files — skills, memories, and other persisted context — are stored as Hub commits, giving you version history on every write and LangSmith-native durability without provisioning a separate LangGraph store.</li>
 </ul></div></div></div>

id="_R_ch5crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>langgraph</code> v1.2.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">This release adds finer-grained control over node execution (timeouts, error recovery, and graceful shutdown), a new channel type that cuts checkpoint overhead for long-running threads, and a new content-block-centric streaming API (v3) with typed, per-channel projections.</span><ul>
 <li>
 <span data-as="p"><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel" class="link"><code>DeltaChannel</code></a> (beta)</strong>: A new channel type that stores only the incremental delta at each step rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value. Most useful for channels that grow large over time, for example a message list in a long-running thread. Use <code>snapshot_frequency=K</code> to write a full snapshot every K steps and bound read latency.</span>
 </li>
@@ -1187,7 +1187,7 @@
 <span data-as="p"><strong>New event streaming API (beta)</strong>: Pass <code>version=&quot;v3&quot;</code> to <code>stream_events()</code> / <code>astream_events()</code> for a content-block-centric protocol with typed, per-channel projections (<code>run.values</code>, <code>run.messages</code>, <code>run.lifecycle</code>, <code>run.subgraphs</code>) plus opt-in transformers for updates, custom events, checkpoints, tasks, and debug. <code>run.messages</code> yields one <code>ChatModelStream</code> per LLM call with typed sub-projections for text, reasoning, tool calls, and usage. <code>version=&quot;v1&quot;</code> and <code>version=&quot;v2&quot;</code> are unchanged.</span>
 </li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Timeouts and error handlers are Python-only; retry policies continue to work in both Python and TypeScript.</span></div></div></div>

 <span data-as="p"><strong>Type-safe streaming (<code>version=&quot;v2&quot;</code>)</strong>: Pass <code>version=&quot;v2&quot;</code> to <code>stream()</code> / <code>astream()</code> for unified <code>StreamPart</code> output with <code>type</code>, <code>ns</code>, and <code>data</code> keys on every chunk. id="_R_c35crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>langchain</code> v1.5.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">LangChain 1.5 adds decision models and decision-driven middleware. This entry also highlights recent improvements across core and integration packages since LangChain 1.4.0.</span><ul>
 <li><strong>Python 3.11 or later required</strong>: <code>langchain</code> 1.5 and <code>langchain-core</code> 1.8 drop Python 3.10 support. Upgrade Python before upgrading these packages.</li>
 <li><strong>Decisions API</strong>: <code>langchain_core.decisions</code> introduces <code>BaseDecisionModel</code> for typed, probabilistic answers about text, messages, and structured state. Questions support binary judgments (<code>Noul</code>), categorical choices (<code>Choice</code>), ordinal scores (<code>Score</code>), and bounding boxes (<code>Box</code>). (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchain/pull/41765" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">langchain#41765</a>)</li>
 <li><strong>Decision-driven middleware (beta)</strong>: <code>ModelRouterMiddleware</code> selects a model for each run from your configured choices and instructions. <code>AutoModeMiddleware</code> classifies configured tool calls and blocks those it judges risky. Versions of these middleware were previously released in <code>langchain-typesafe</code>. They now require an explicit decision model through <code>classifier=</code>. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchain/pull/41766" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">langchain#41766</a>)</li>
@@ -1126,7 +1126,7 @@
 <li><strong>Summarization improvements</strong>: <code>SummarizationMiddleware</code> accepts a separate model or custom callable through <code>summarizer=</code>.</li>
 <li><strong>Mid-conversation updates</strong>: Supported models accept <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/anthropic#mid-conversation-system-messages" class="link">Anthropic system and tool updates</a>, <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/openai#add-tools-mid-conversation" class="link">OpenAI Responses tool additions</a>, and <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/openai#changing-reasoning-effort-mid-conversation" class="link">OpenAI reasoning-effort changes</a> without restarting the conversation.</li>
 </ul></div></div></div>

v1.7.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">In <a href="/oss/python/langchain/event-streaming" class="link">event streaming</a>, tool-call arguments now stream as <code>tool-call-delta</code> events. Each event carries only the new part of the arguments, as <code>text-delta</code> does for text. Earlier versions sent <code>block-delta</code> events that repeated all of the arguments so far.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="upgrade-clients-before-the-server" aria-labelledby="_R_s55crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#upgrade-clients-before-the-server" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Upgrade clients before the server">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_s55crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Upgrade clients before the server</span></h3><span data-as="p">Clients older than the versions below do not handle <code>tool-call-delta</code>. For example, earlier versions of <code>@langchain/react</code> can stop updating a <code>useMessages</code> view. The <a href="/langsmith/agent-server" class="link">Agent Server</a> passes events through unchanged, so the <code>langchain-core</code> version on the server decides which events clients receive.</span><ol>

Earlier versions sent <code>block-delta</code> events that repeated all of the arguments so far.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="upgrade-clients-before-the-server" aria-labelledby="_R_s55crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#upgrade-clients-before-the-server" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Upgrade clients before the server">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_s55crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Upgrade clients before the server</span></h3><span data-as="p">Clients older than the versions below do not handle <code>tool-call-delta</code>. For example, earlier versions of <code>@langchain/react</code> can stop updating a <code>useMessages</code> view. The <a href="/langsmith/agent-server" class="link">Agent Server</a> passes events through unchanged, so the <code>langchain-core</code> version on the server decides which events clients receive.</span><ol>
 <li>Upgrade every client that reads the stream:
 <ul>
 <li>In JavaScript, upgrade <code>@langchain/react</code>, <code>@langchain/vue</code>, <code>@langchain/svelte</code>, or <code>@langchain/angular</code> to 1.2.6 or later.</li>
@@ -1135,13 +1135,13 @@
 </li>
 <li>Upgrade <code>langchain-core</code> on the server to 1.7.0 or later, together with <code>langchain</code> 1.4.7 or later.</li>
 </ol><span data-as="p"><code>langchain</code> and <code>deepagents</code> accept any <code>langchain-core</code> 1.x, so upgrading either one can pull in 1.7.0. To hold the server back until you upgrade every client, pin <code>langchain-core&lt;1.7.0</code>.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="breaking-changes" aria-labelledby="_R_1s55crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#breaking-changes" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Breaking changes">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1s55crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Breaking changes</span></h3><span data-as="p">Starting in <code>langchain-core</code> 1.6.13, iterating <code>message.tool_calls</code> yields only the new part of each tool call’s arguments, with the call’s <code>id</code> and <code>name</code>. Earlier versions yielded all of the arguments so far. A call’s chunks concatenate into its full arguments, and <code>message.tool_calls.get()</code> still returns the parsed tool calls.</span></div></div></div>

 <li><strong>Typed answers</strong>: each interrupt carries a <code>response_schema</code> listing the decisions that call allows, so a client can build its form from the schema. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#read-the-response-schema" class="link">Read the response schema</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Checked before saving</strong>: LangGraph rejects an invalid answer before it is saved, so you can correct it and answer the same interrupt again. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#correct-an-invalid-answer" class="link">Correct an invalid answer</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Edits stay on the called tool</strong>: an <code>edit</code> can’t switch tools, and its arguments are checked against the tool’s own schema.</li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Use per-call mode for new agents. LangChain plans to make it the default in the next major release. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#pause-once-per-tool-call" class="link">Pause once per tool call</a> and the <a href="/oss/python/concepts/human-in-the-loop" class="link">human-in-the-loop overview</a>.</span></div></div></div>

Importing from it raises a <code>LangChainBetaWarning</code>.</span></div></div><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-2" aria-labelledby="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features-2" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>One adapter, any transport</strong>: <code>MCPAdapter</code> infers the transport from its target — a URL, a local script over stdio, an in-process server, an <code>MCPConfig</code> dict for <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/connections#multiple-servers" class="link">multiple servers</a>, or a pre-built <code>fastmcp.Client</code>. <code>await adapter.list_tools()</code> returns LangChain tools ready for <code>create_agent</code>. See the <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp" class="link">MCP overview</a>.</li>
@@ -1149,7 +1149,7 @@
 <li><strong>Authentication through FastMCP</strong>: bearer tokens, full OAuth 2.1 with dynamic client registration, or any <code>httpx.Auth</code>, including <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth#per-user-authentication" class="link">per-user authentication</a> in a deployment. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth" class="link">Authentication</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Richer tool metadata</strong>: each tool carries its MCP provenance under an <code>mcp</code> namespace on its metadata, including annotations such as <code>destructive_hint</code> for <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#human-in-the-loop" class="link">gating destructive tools behind approval</a>. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#tool-metadata" class="link">Tool metadata</a>.</li>
 </ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="migrating" aria-labelledby="_R_1s95crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#migrating" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Migrating">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1s95crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Migrating</span></h3><span data-as="p">Moving from <code>langchain-mcp-adapters</code>? <code>MultiServerMCPClient</code> is replaced by <code>MCPAdapter</code>, and several features have changed or been removed. See the <a href="/oss/python/migrate/langchain-mcp-adapters" class="link">migration guide</a>.</span></div></div></div>

id="_R_cb5crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>deepagents</code> v0.7.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">A leaner, more configurable harness by default. On a default-agent turn, input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895), validated against our <a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/how-we-benchmark-deep-agents" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">revamped evaluation suite</a> with no quality regression.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="optimizations" aria-labelledby="_R_sb5crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#optimizations" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Optimizations">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_sb5crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Optimizations</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>Lean prompts by default</strong>: The authored base prompt starts empty and tool-usage prose that duplicated tool schemas has been trimmed. Isolated to the default agent’s tool schemas, total description tokens drop <strong>43%</strong> (4,005 → 2,302); combined with the empty base prompt and opt-in todos, a default-agent turn’s input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895). Tool behavior is unchanged. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4859" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4859</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4979" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4979</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/5009" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#5009</a>)</li>
 </ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-3" aria-labelledby="_R_1cb5crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features-3" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1cb5crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/customization#middleware" class="link">Override a default middleware instance</a></strong>: A <code>middleware=</code> (or subagent <code>middleware</code>) instance whose <code>.name</code> matches a built-in now replaces that default in place, rather than erroring on a duplicate. For example, pass your own <code>SummarizationMiddleware(...)</code> to change the token trigger or summary model without disabling the built-in default. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4251" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4251</a>)</li>
@@ -1161,7 +1161,7 @@
 <li><strong>Backend compatibility shims removed</strong>: Pass concrete <code>BackendProtocol</code> instances instead of factories, configure <code>StoreBackend</code> with an explicit <code>namespace</code>, and use the current <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> / <code>grep</code> / <code>ReadResult</code> APIs. Removed symbols include <code>BackendFactory</code>, <code>BACKEND_TYPES</code>, <code>FileFormat</code>, and <code>Unset</code>. New files store string <code>FileData.content</code>; older <code>list[str]</code> content stays readable and converts on next write. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4541" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4541</a>)</li>
 <li><strong>Output format changes</strong>: Empty <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> output is now <code>No files found</code> instead of <code>[]</code>, and <code>read_file</code> no longer renders a fixed-width <code>cat -n</code>-style gutter — update any parsers of raw tool output. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4561" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4561</a>)</li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Copy the following prompt into your AI coding assistant to migrate a codebase for these breaking changes:</span><div class="prompt my-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-4 rounded-2xl border p-4 border-stone-100 bg-stone-50 dark:border-stone-800 dark:bg-stone-900 text-sm/[20px] text-stone-900 tracking-[-0.1px] dark:text-stone-50 print:print-color-exact"><div class="not-prose flex min-w-0 items-start gap-2" data-component-part="prompt-content-wrapper"><span aria-hidden="true" class="flex h-[1lh] shrink-0 select-none items-center justify-center" data-component-part="prompt-icon"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! id="_R_cd5crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>deepagents</code> v0.6.0</span></h2><ul>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/interpreters" class="link"><code>CodeInterpreterMiddleware</code></a></strong>: (experimental) <code>deepagents</code> now supports code execution and programmatic tool calling through a scoped QuickJS runtime.</li>
 <li>Supports <code>version=&quot;v3&quot;</code> in <code>stream_events</code> / <code>astream_events</code>. Refer to the <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/event-streaming" class="link">event streaming</a> guide for details.</li>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel" class="link"><code>DeltaChannel</code></a> (beta)</strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/delta-channels-evolving-agent-runtime" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">blog</a>): Deep Agents now uses <code>DeltaChannel</code> for message history and agent files. Rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value into every checkpoint, only the incremental delta written at each step is stored — keeping checkpoint sizes small as threads grow long.</li>
@@ -1169,8 +1169,8 @@
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/profiles" class="link">Harness profiles</a></strong>: Register per-provider or per-model configuration bundles (<code>HarnessProfile</code>) that <code>create_deep_agent</code> applies automatically when a model is selected — system-prompt tweaks, tool overrides, middleware changes, and subagent defaults — without modifying the call site.</li>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/backends#contexthubbackend" class="link"><code>ContextHubBackend</code></a></strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/introducing-context-hub" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">blog</a>): A new filesystem backend backed by LangSmith Hub. Agent files — skills, memories, and other persisted context — are stored as Hub commits, giving you version history on every write and LangSmith-native durability without provisioning a separate LangGraph store.</li>
 </ul></div></div></div>

id="_R_ch5crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>langgraph</code> v1.2.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">This release adds finer-grained control over node execution (timeouts, error recovery, and graceful shutdown), a new channel type that cuts checkpoint overhead for long-running threads, and a new content-block-centric streaming API (v3) with typed, per-channel projections.</span><ul>
 <li>
 <span data-as="p"><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel" class="link"><code>DeltaChannel</code></a> (beta)</strong>: A new channel type that stores only the incremental delta at each step rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value. Most useful for channels that grow large over time, for example a message list in a long-running thread. Use <code>snapshot_frequency=K</code> to write a full snapshot every K steps and bound read latency.</span>
 </li>
@@ -1187,7 +1187,7 @@
 <span data-as="p"><strong>New event streaming API (beta)</strong>: Pass <code>version=&quot;v3&quot;</code> to <code>stream_events()</code> / <code>astream_events()</code> for a content-block-centric protocol with typed, per-channel projections (<code>run.values</code>, <code>run.messages</code>, <code>run.lifecycle</code>, <code>run.subgraphs</code>) plus opt-in transformers for updates, custom events, checkpoints, tasks, and debug. <code>run.messages</code> yields one <code>ChatModelStream</code> per LLM call with typed sub-projections for text, reasoning, tool calls, and usage. <code>version=&quot;v1&quot;</code> and <code>version=&quot;v2&quot;</code> are unchanged.</span>
 </li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Timeouts and error handlers are Python-only; retry policies continue to work in both Python and TypeScript.</span></div></div></div>
